# The Ultimate Free API Mega List

**3,269 free APIs · live-tested and working · 57 categories · verified 2026-10-12**

[![Donate](https://img.shields.io/badge/Donate-Support%20Development-28a745?style=for-the-badge&logo=githubsponsors&logoColor=white)](https://cpubyte-donate.blogspot.com/)

[![Buy Me a Coffee](https://img.shields.io/badge/Buy_Me_a_Coffee-Support_My_Work-FFDD00?style=for-the-badge&logo=buymeacoffee&logoColor=black)](https://cpubyte-donate.blogspot.com/)

</div>

Every API below returned a live response when tested and is usable at no cost (fully open or with a free plan — no credit card required). Auth: **No** = open, no key · **apiKey** = free signup key · **OAuth** = free tier via OAuth · **basic** = free tier via basic auth. API names are clickable links to the official documentation.
